# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
1. Position is a lexeme that would be mapped into a token <id, 1>, where id is an abstract symbol standing for an identifier and 1 points to the symbol-table entry for position. The symbol-table entry for an identifier holds information about the identifier, such as its name and types.

2. The assignment symbol = is a lexeme that is mapped into a token <=>. This token needs no attribute value.

3. initial is a lexeme that would be mapped into <id, 2>, where 2 points to the symbol table entry for  initial.

4.  + to <+>

5. rate ---> <id, 3>

6. * to <*>

7. 60 ---> <60> ^4c4LxF6m
